Build Failure Analysis for google/auto\n\n## Repository Location\n/home/travis/build/failed/google/auto\n\n## Environment\n- Java Version: OpenJDK 11.0.27\n- Build System: Maven\n\n## Root Cause Analysis\n\n### Primary Issue: tools.jar Dependency Incompatibility with Java 11+\n\nThe build fails with the following error:\n```\nCould not find artifact com.sun:tools:jar:11.0.27 at specified path /usr/lib/jvm/java-11-openjdk-amd64/../lib/tools.jar\n```\n\n#### Explanation:\n- In Java 8 and earlier, `tools.jar` was a separate JAR file located in `$JAVA_HOME/lib/tools.jar`\n- Starting with Java 9, `tools.jar` was removed and its contents were modularized into the JDK modules\n- The project's test dependencies (`compile-testing` library versions 0.6, 0.7, and 0.8) have a hard dependency on `com.sun:tools` with a system scope pointing to the non-existent `tools.jar`\n\n#### Affected Modules:\n1. `common/pom.xml` - uses `compile-testing:0.7` (test scope)\n2. `value/pom.xml` - uses `compile-testing:0.6` (test scope)\n3. `service/pom.xml` - uses `compile-testing:0.6` (test scope)\n4. `factory/pom.xml` - uses `compile-testing:0.8` (test scope)\n\n### Secondary Issue: Java Source/Target Version\n\nThe parent pom.xml specifies Java 1.6 as the source and target version:\n```xml\n<java.version>1.6</java.version>\n```\n\nThis is incompatible with Java 11+ compiler which no longer supports targeting Java 1.6.\n\n## Proposed Fixes\n\n### Fix 1: Update compile-testing dependency versions\nUpdate the `compile-testing` library to version 0.18 or later, which uses a conditional profile for `tools.jar` that only activates when the file actually exists.\n\nFiles to modify:\n- `common/pom.xml`: Change `compile-testing` version from `0.7` to `0.18`\n- version from `0.8` to `0.18`\n\n### Fix 2: Update Java version in parent pom.xml\nUpdate the Java version from 1.6 to 1.8 (the minimum version supported by Java 11 compiler for source/target):\n\nFile to modify:\n- `pom.xml`: Change `<java.version>1.6</java.version>` to `<java.version>1.8</java.version>`\n\n### Additional Consideration: Truth library compatibility\nThe `compile-testing:0.18` requires `truth` version 0.42 or later.
